bg-zinc-800/50 border border-zinc-700 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 text-white transition-colors appearance-none"\n\n              >\n\n                <option value="" disabled selected>Select an option...</option>\n\n                <option value="Google Search">Google Search</option>\n\n                <option value="Word of Mouth">Word of Mouth / Referral</option>\n\n                <option value="Instagram">Instagram</option>\n\n                <option value="Facebook">Facebook</option>\n\n                <option value="Local Directory">Local Directory</option>\n\n                <option value="Other">Other</option>\n\n              </select>\n\n            </div>\n\n\n\n            <div>\n\n              <label\n\n                for="message"\n\n                class="block text-sm font-medium text-zinc-300 mb-2"\n\n                >Project Details</label\n\n              >\n\n              <textarea\n\n                id="message"\n\n                name="message"\n\n                rows="5"\n\n                required\n\n                placeholder="Tell us about the location, timeline, and what you\'re looking to capture..."\n\n                class="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-zinc-800/50 border border-zinc-700 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 text-white placeholder-zinc-500 transition-colors resize-none"\n\n              ></textarea>\n\n            </div>\n\n\n\n            <button\n\n              type="submit"\n\n              id="submitBtn"\n\n              class="w-full bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white font-bold py-4 px-4 rounded-lg transition-all duration-300"\n\n            >\n\n              Send Inquiry\n\n            </button>\n\n          </form>\n\n          <div id="formStatus" class="mt-6 text-center font-bold hidden"></div>\n\n        </div>\n\n      </div>\n\n    </section>\n\n\n\n    <footer\n\n      class="flex items-center justify-around bg-zinc-900 text-white p-4 border-t border-zinc-800 mt-auto"\n\n    >\n\n      <p>© 2026 Drone Company. All rights reserved.</p>\n\n      <div class="flex space-x-4">\n\n        <a\n\n          href="/privacy"\n\n          class="text-zinc-300 hover:text-white transition-colors"\n\n          >Privacy</a\n\n        >\n\n        <a\n\n          href="/terms"\n\n          class="text-zinc-300 hover:text-white transition-colors"\n\n          >Terms</a\n\n        >\n\n      </div>\n\n    </footer>\n\n\n\n    <script>\n\n      const form = document.getElementById("contactForm");\n\n      const submitBtn = document.getElementById("submitBtn");\n\n      const statusDiv = document.getElementById("formStatus");\n\n\n\n      if (form) {\n\n        form.addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {\n\n          e.preventDefault();\n\n          submitBtn.textContent = "Sending...";\n\n          submitBtn.disabled = true;\n\n          statusDiv.classList.add("hidden");\n\n\n\n          const formData = new FormData(form);\n\n          const data = Object.fromEntries(formData.entries());\n\n\n\n          try {\n\n            const response = await fetch("/api/contact", {\n\n              method: "POST",\n\n              headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n\n              body: JSON.stringify(data),\n\n            });\n\n\n\n            const result = await response.json();\n\n\n\n            if (response.ok) {\n\n              statusDiv.textContent =\n\n                "Message sent successfully! We will be in touch shortly.";\n\n              statusDiv.className = "mt-6 text-center font-bold text-green-400";\n\n              statusDiv.classList.remove("hidden");\n\n              form.reset();\n\n            } else {\n\n              throw new Error(result.error || "Failed to send message");\n\n            }\n\n          } catch (error) {\n\n            console.error("Form error:", error);\n\n            statusDiv.textContent =\n\n              error.message || "An error occurred. Please try again.";\n\n            statusDiv.className = "mt-6 text-center font-bold text-red-400";\n\n            statusDiv.classList.remove("hidden");\n\n          } finally {\n\n            submitBtn.textContent = "Send Inquiry";\n\n            submitBtn.disabled = false;\n\n          }\n\n        });\n\n      }\n\n    </script>\n\n  </body>\n\n</html>\n=== END contact.html ===\n\n\n=== js/main.js ===\n// Smooth scrolling for anchor links\n\ndocument.querySelectorAll(\'a[href^="#"]\').forEach((anchor) => {\n\n  anchor.addEventListener("click", function (e) {\n\n    e.preventDefault();\n\n\n\n    const target = document.querySelector(this.getAttribute("href"));\n\n    if (target) {\n\n      window.scrollTo({\n\n        top: target.offsetTop - 70,\n\n        behavior: "smooth",\n\n      });\n\n    }\n\n  });\n\n});\n\n\n\n// Header scroll effect\n\nwindow.addEventListener("scroll", function () {\n\n  const header = document.querySelector(".header");\n\n  if (header) {\n\n    if (window.scrollY > 100) {\n\n      header.classList.add("scrolled");\n\n    } else {\n\n      header.classList.remove("scrolled");\n\n    }\n\n  }\n\n});\n\n\n\n// Portfolio gallery animation on scroll\n\nconst portfolioItems = document.querySelectorAll(".portfolio-item");\n\n\n\nif (portfolioItems.length > 0) {\n\n  const observer = new IntersectionObserver(\n\n    (entries) => {\n\n      entries.forEach((entry) => {\n\n        if (entry.isIntersecting) {\n\n          entry.target.style.opacity = 1;\n\n          entry.target.style.transform = "translateY(0)";\n\n        }\n\n      });\n\n    },\n\n    { threshold: 0.1 },\n\n  );\n\n\n\n  portfolioItems.forEach((item) => {\n\n    item.style.opacity = 0;\n\n    item.style.transform = "translateY(20px)";\n\n    item.style.transition = "opacity 0.5s ease, transform 0.5s ease";\n\n    observer.observe(item);\n\n  });\n\n}\n\n\n\n// Add video hover functionality\n\ndocument.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {\n\n  const videoCards = document.querySelectorAll(".video-card");\n\n\n\n  videoCards.forEach((card) => {\n\n    const video = card.querySelector("video");\n\n\n\n    if (video) {\n\n      // Play video on hover\n\n      card.addEventListener("mouseenter", () => {\n\n        try {\n\n          const playPromise = video.play();\n\n\n\n          if (playPromise !== undefined) {\n\n            playPromise.catch((e) => {\n\n              console.log("Video play failed (autoplay policy):", e);\n\n              // If it fails due to autoplay restrictions, try muted version\n\n              if (!video.muted) {\n\n                video.muted = true;\n\n                video.play().catch((err) => {\n\n                  console.error("Failed even with muted:", err);\n\n                });\n\n              }\n\n            });\n\n          }\n\n        } catch (error) {\n\n          console.error("Error playing video:", error);\n\n        }\n\n      });\n\n\n\n      // Pause video and reset to poster on mouse leave\n\n      card.addEventListener("mouseleave", () => {\n\n        video.pause();\n\n        // video.load() resets the element and forces the poster to display again.\n\n        // Using video.currentTime = 0 can cause a black frame depending on the codec.\n\n        video.load();\n\n      });\n\n\n\n      // Handle video errors\n\n      video.addEventListener("error", () => {\n\n        console.error("Error loading video");\n\n      });\n\n    }\n\n  });\n\n});\n\n\n\n// Form submission handling (Only active on pages with the contact form)\n\nconst contactForm = document.getElementById("contactForm");\n\nif (contactForm) {\n\n  contactForm.addEventListener("submit", async function (e) {\n\n    e.preventDefault();\n\n\n\n    // Get form data using FormData and convert to plain object\n\n    const formData = new FormData(this);\n\n    const data = {\n\n      name: formData.get("name"),\n\n      email: formData.get("email"),\n\n      subject: formData.get("subject"),\n\n      message: formData.get("message"),\n\n    };\n\n\n\n    try {\n\n      // Show loading state\n\n      const submitButton = this.querySelector(\'button[type="submit"]\');\n\n      const originalText = submitButton.textContent;\n\n      submitButton.disabled = true;\n\n      submitButton.textContent = "Sending...";\n\n\n\n      // Send data to API endpoint\n\n      const response = await fetch("/api/contact", {\n\n        method: "POST",\n\n        headers: {\n\n          "Content-Type": "application/json",\n\n        },\n\n        body: JSON.stringify(data),\n\n      });\n\n\n\n      // Reset loading state\n\n      submitButton.disabled = false;\n\n      submitButton.textContent = originalText;\n\n\n\n      const result = await response.json();\n\n\n\n      if (response.ok) {\n\n        // Show success message\n\n        showMessage(\n\n          "Message sent successfully! We will get back to you soon.",\n\n          "success",\n\n        );\n\n        this.reset();\n\n      } else {\n\n        // Show error message\n\n        console.error("Server error:", result.error);\n\n        showMessage(\n\n          result.error || "Failed to send message. Please try again.",\n\n          "error",\n\n        );\n\n      }\n\n    } catch (error) {\n\n      // Reset loading state\n\n      const submitButton = this.querySelector(\'button[type="submit"]\');\n\n      submitButton.disabled = false;\n\n      submitButton.textContent = "Send Inquiry";\n\n\n\n      console.error("Error sending contact form:", error);\n\n      showMessage(\n\n        "Network error. Please check your connection and try again.",\n\n        "error",\n\n      );\n\n    }\n\n  });\n\n\n\n  // Function to display messages\n\n  function showMessage(message, type) {\n\n    const messageContainer = document.getElementById("formStatus");\n\n    if (!messageContainer) return;\n\n\n\n    messageContainer.innerHTML = `\n\n            <div class="p-4 rounded-md ${type === "success" ? "bg-green-900/50 text-green-400 border border-green-800" : "bg-red-900/50 text-red-400 border border-red-800"}">\n\n                <p>${message}</p>\n\n            </div>\n\n        `;\n\n\n\n    messageContainer.classList.remove("hidden");\n\n\n\n    // Auto-hide success messages after 5 seconds\n\n    if (type === "success") {\n\n      setTimeout(() => {\n\n        messageContainer.classList.add("hidden");\n\n      }, 5000);\n\n    }\n\n  }\n\n}\n\n\n=== END js/main.js ===\n\n\n=== index.html ===\n<!doctype html>\n\n<html lang="en">\n\n  <head>\n\n    <!-- Compiled Tailwind CSS -->\n\n    <link rel="stylesheet" href="/dist/tailwind.css" />\n\n\n\n    <!-- Inter Font for Premium Typography -->\n\n    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com" />\n\n    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin />\n\n    <link\n\n      href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@300;400;500;600;700&display=swap"\n\n      rel="stylesheet"\n\n    />\n\n    <title>Request a Quote - Drone Company</title>\n\n  </head>\n\n\n\n  <body class="bg-zinc-950 text-zinc-100 font-sans">\n\n    <!-- Navigation Bar (using Tailwind classes) -->\n\n    <nav class="bg-zinc-900 border-b border-zinc-800">\n\n      <div\n\n        class="max-w-3xl mx-auto grid grid-cols-[1fr_auto_1fr] items-center p-4"\n\n      >\n\n        <a href="/" class="text-lg font-bold text-blue-500 justify-self-start"\n\n          >Drone Company</a\n\n        >\n\n\n\n        <div id="navMenu" class="hidden md:flex justify-center space-x-8">\n\n          <a\n\n            href="/about"\n\n            class="hover:text-white transition-colors duration-300 ease-in-out transform hover:scale-105"\n\n            >Services</a\n\n          >\n\n          <a\n\n            href="/portfolio"\n\n            class="hover:text-white transition-colors duration-300 ease-in-out transform hover:scale-105"\n\n            >Portfolio</a\n\n          >\n\n        </div>\n\n\n\n        <a\n\n          href="/contact"\n\n          class="justify-self-end hover:text-white transition-colors duration-300 ease-in-out transform hover:scale-105"\n\n          >Request a Quote</a\n\n        >\n\n      </div>\n\n    </nav>\n\n\n\n    <main\n\n      class="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center relative overflow-hidden bg-black"\n\n    >\n\n      <!-- Background video -->\n\n      <video\n\n        class="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover"\n\n        src="/assets/optimized/cinewhoop_creative_edit_001_web.mp4"\n\n        type="video/mp4"\n\n aria-hidden="true"\n\n      ></video>\n\n\n\n      <!-- Gradient overlay -->\n\n      <div\n\n        class="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-zinc-950 to-transparent z-10"\n\n      ></div>\n\n\n\n      <!-- Content -->\n\n      <div class="text-center p-4 relative z-20">\n\n        <h1\n\n          id="heroText"\n\n          class="text-5xl md:text-6xl font-extrabold text-white mb-6 tracking-tight"\n\n        >\n\n          Elevate your brand. Literally.\n\n        </h1>\n\n        <p class="text-xl text-zinc-300 mb-8 max-w-2xl mx-auto">\n\n          Choreographed one-shot flythroughs · Virtual tours · Aerial exteriors\n\n          · Construction progress · Inspection · Events · FPV cinematic\n\n          <br /><br /><br />\n\n          Part 107 certified · Fully insured · 4K delivery · Expedited\n\n          turnaround available\n\n        </p>\n\n        <a\n\n          href="/contact"\n\n          class="inline-block bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white rounded-full py-3 px-8 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out transform hover:scale-105 hover:shadow-lg hover:shadow-blue-500/20"\n\n        >\n\n          Contact Us\n\n        </a\n\n      </div>\n\n    </main>\n\n\n\n    <!-- Why Choose Us Section -->\n\n    <section id="choose-us" class="py-16 px-4">\n\n      <div class="max-w-7xl mx-auto">\n\n        <div class="text-center mb-12">\n\n          <h2 class="text-4xl font-bold text-white mb-4">\n\n            Precision Aerial Solutions for Discerning Brands\n\n          </h2>\n\n          <p class="text-xl text-zinc-300 max-w-2xl mx-auto">\n\n            Crafting cinematic aerial experiences that captivate audiences and\n\n            elevate your brand\'s visual narrative. Our pilots execute high-stakes maneuvers with surgical precision, capturing fluid, immersive sequences that redefine the boundaries of visual storytelling.\n\n            </p>\n\n          </div>\n\n\n\n          <!-- Value Proposition 2 -->\n\n          <div\n\n            class="bg-zinc-900 rounded-xl p-6 border border-zinc-800 transition-all duration-300 hover:border-blue-500/50 hover:shadow-lg hover:shadow-blue-500/10"\n\n          >\n\n            <h3 class="text-xl font-bold text-white mb-4 flex items-center">\n\n              <span>Bespoke Production</span>\n\n            </h3>\n\n            <p class="text-zinc-300 leading-relaxed">\n\n              From initial concept to high-resolution masterpiece. We orchestrate every facet of production, providing a frictionless, turn-key experience that empowers your brand\'s unique narrative.\n\n            </p>\n\n          </div>\n\n\n\n          <!-- Value Proposition 3 -->\n\n          <div\n\n            class="bg-zinc-900 rounded-xl p-6 border border-zinc-800 transition-all duration-300 hover:border-blue-500/50 hover:shadow-lg hover:shadow-blue-500/10"\n\n          >\n\n            <h3 class="text-xl font-bold text-white mb-4 flex items-center">\n\n              <span>Secure Operations</span>\n\n            </h3>\n\n            <p class="text-zinc-300 leading-relaxed">\n\n              Navigate the skies with absolute confidence. Fully FAA-certified and insured, we manage all regulatory complexities to ensure your production remains seamless, safe, and uncompromisingly professional.\n\n            </p>\n\n          </div>\n\n        </div>\n\n      </div>\n\n    </section>\n\n\n\n    <!-- Footer (using Tailwind classes) -->\n\n    <footer\n\n      class="flex items-center justify-around bg-zinc-900 text-white p-4 border-t border-zinc-800"\n\n    >\n\n      <p>© 2026 Drone Company. All rights reserved.</p>\n\n      <div class="flex space-x-4">\n\n        <a\n\n          href="/privacy"\n\n          class="text-zinc-300 hover:text-white transition-colors"\n\n          >Privacy</a\n\n        >\n\n        <a\n\n          href="/terms"\n\n          class="text-zinc-300 hover:text-white transition-colors"\n\n          >Terms</a\n\n        >\n\n        <a\n\n          href="/contact"\n\n          class="text-zinc-300 hover:text-white transition-colors"\n\n          >Contact</a\n\n        >\n\n      </div>\n\n    </footer>\n\n  </body>\n\n  <script>\n\n    // Active link detection\n\n    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {\n\n      const currentPage = window.location.pathname.split("/").pop();\n\n      const navLinks = document.querySelectorAll("#navMenu a");\n\n\n\n      navLinks.forEach((link) => {\n\n        if (link.getAttribute("href") === currentPage) {\n\n          link.classList.add("active-link");\n\n        }\n\n      });\n\n    });\n\n  </script>\n\n</html>\n=== END index.html ===\n\n\n=== server.js ===\nrequire("dotenv").config();\n\nconst express = require("express");\n\nconst path = require("path");\n\nconst fs = require("fs");\n\nconst helmet = require("helmet");\n\nconst session = require("express-session");\n\nconst Database = require("better-sqlite3");\n\nconst SQLiteStore = require("better-sqlite3-session-store")(session);\n\nconst argon2 = require("argon2");\n\nconst rateLimit = require("express-rate-limit");\n\nconst speakeasy = require("speakeasy");\n\nconst qrcode = require("qrcode");\n\nconst db = require("./db");\n\nconst { jsonToCsv } = require("./lib/csv-helper");\n\nconst { SCHEMA } = require("./lib/schema");\n\nconst { buildQuery } = require("./lib/query-builder");\n\nconst { runRawQuery } = require("./lib/sql-sandbox");\n\n\n\nconst app = express();\n\nconst PORT = process.env.PORT || 3000;\n\n\n\napp.set("trust proxy", 1);\n\napp.use(helmet({ contentSecurityPolicy: false }));\n\n\n\nconst loginLimiter = rateLimit({\n\n  windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000,\n\n  max: 10,\n\n  message: { error: "Too many attempts, try again later." },\n\n});\n\nconst contactLimiter = rateLimit({\n\n  windowMs: 60 * 60 * 1000,\n\n  max: 5,\n\n  message: { error: "Too many submissions." },\n\n});\n\n\n\nconst sessionDb = new Database(path.join(__dirname, "data", "sessions.db"));\n\n\n\napp.use(\n\n  session({\n\n    store: new SQLiteStore({\n\n      client: sessionDb,\n\n      expired: {\n\n        clear: true,\n\n        intervalMs: 900000, // 15 minutes\n\n      },\n\n    }),\n\n    secret: process.env.SESSION_SECRET || "local-dev-fallback-secret",\n\n    resave: false,\n\n    saveUninitialized: false,\n\n    cookie: { secure: false, httpOnly: true, maxAge: 86400000 },\n\n  }),\n\n);\n\n\n\napp.use(express.json({ limit: "1mb" }));\n\napp.use(express.urlencoded({ extended: true }));\n\n\n\nfunction requireAuth(req, res, next) {\n\n  if (req.session && req.session.userId) return next();\n\n  if (req.path.startsWith("/api/"))\n\n    return res.status(401).json({ error: "Unauthorized" });\n\n  res.redirect("/admin");\n\n}\n\n\n\n// Only the "owner" role can create, reset, or remove other admin accounts.\n\n// Alpha testers and other admins created through the API get the "admin" role\n\n// and never pass this check, even though they\'re fully authenticated.\n\nfunction requireOwner(req, res, next) {\n\n  const user = db.findUserById(req.session.userId);\n\n  if (!user || user.role !== "owner")\n\n    return res.status(403).json({ error: "Forbidden: owner role required" });\n\n  next();\n\n}\n\n\n\n// Accounts created or reset through the admin UI are issued a one-time temporary\n\n// password and flagged must_change_password. Until they set their own password,\n\n// every admin API route is blocked except the two needed to clear that flag.\n\nconst PASSWORD_CHANGE_EXEMPT_PATHS = new Set([\n\n  "/api/admin/profile",\n\n  "/api/admin/change-password",\n\n]);\n\nfunction blockUntilPasswordChanged(req, res, next) {\n\n  if (\n\n    req.session &&\n\n    req.session.userId &&\n\n    req.path.startsWith("/api/admin/") &&\n\n    !PASSWORD_CHANGE_EXEMPT_PATHS.has(req.path)\n\n  ) {\n\n    const user = db.findUserById(req.session.userId);\n\n    if (user && user.must_change_password) {\n\n      return res.status(403).json({\n\n        error: "You must set a new password before continuing.",\n\n        code: "PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED",\n\n      });\n\n    }\n\n  }\n\n  next();\n\n}\n\napp.use(blockUntilPasswordChanged);\n\n\n\nfunction normalizeRecoveryCode(code) {\n\n  if (!code) return "";\n\n  return code.toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9]/g, "");\n\n}\n\n\n\napp.use("/dist", express.static(path.join(__dirname, "dist")));\n\napp.use("/assets", express.static(path.join(__dirname, "assets")));\n\napp.use("/js", express.static(path.join(__dirname, "js")));\n\n\n\napp.get("/", (req, res) => res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, "index.html")));\n\napp.get("/about", (req, res) =>\n\n  res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, "about.html")),\n\n);\n\napp.get("/services", (req, res) =>\n\n  res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, "services.html")),\n\n);\n\napp.get("/portfolio", (req, res) =>\n\n  res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, "portfolio.html")),\n\n);\n\napp.get("/contact", (req, res) =>\n\n  res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, "contact.html")),\n\n);\n\napp.get("/privacy", (req, res) =>\n\n  res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, "privacy.html")),\n\n);\n\napp.get("/terms", (req, res) =>\n\n  res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, "terms.html")),\n\n);\n\napp.get("/admin", (req, res) =>\n\n  res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, "admin.html")),\n\n);\n\n\n\n// --- AUTH ---\n\napp.post("/api/auth/login", loginLimiter, async (req, res) => {\n\n  try {\n\n    const { username, password } = req.body;\n\n    const user = db.findUserByUsername(username);\n\n    if (!user) return res.status(401).json({ error: "Invalid credentials" });\n\n    const ok = await argon2.verify(user.password_hash, password);\n\n    if (!ok) return res.status(401).json({ error: "Invalid credentials" });\n\n    req.session.userId = user.id;\n\n    db.updateLastLogin(user.id);\n\n    res.status(200).json({\n\n      success: true,\n\n      mustChangePassword: !!user.must_change_password,\n\n    });\n\n  } catch (e) {\n\n    res.status(500).json({ error: "Internal server error" });\n\n  }\n\n});\n\n\n\napp.post("/api/auth/logout", (req, res) =>\n\n  req.session.destroy(() => res.status(200).json({ success: true })),\n\n);\n\n\n\napp.post("/api/auth/check-verification", loginLimiter, (req, res) => {\n\n  try {\n\n    const { username } = req.body;\n\n    if (!username) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Username required" });\n\n    const user = db.findUserByUsername(username);\n\n    if (!user) return res.json({ method: "recovery" });\n\n    if (user.totp_secret) return res.json({ method: "totp" });\n\n    const unused = db.getUnusedRecoveryCodes(user.id);\n\n    if (unused.length === 0) return res.json({ method: "none" });\n\n    return res.json({ method: "recovery", remaining: unused.length });\n\n  } catch (e) {\n\n    res.status(500).json({ error: "Internal server error" });\n\n  }\n\n});\n\n\n\napp.post("/api/auth/reset-password", loginLimiter, async (req, res) => {\n\n  try {\n\n    const { username, newPassword, totpToken, recoveryCode } = req.body;\n\n    if (!username || !newPassword)\n\n      return res\n\n        .status(400)\n\n        .json({ error: "Username and new password required" });\n\n    if (newPassword.length < 8)\n\n      return res\n\n        .status(400)\n\n        .json({ error: "Password must be at least 8 characters" });\n\n    const user = db.findUserByUsername(username);\n\n    if (!user)\n\n      return res.status(400).json({ error: "Unable to reset password" });\n\n\n\n    if (user.totp_secret) {\n\n      if (!totpToken)\n\n        return res.status(400).json({ error: "2FA code required" });\n\n      const ok = speakeasy.totp.verify({\n\n        secret: user.totp_secret,\n\n        encoding: "base32",\n\n        token: totpToken,\n\n        window: 1,\n\n      });\n\n      if (!ok) return res.status(401).json({ error: "Invalid 2FA code" });\n\n    } else {\n\n      if (!recoveryCode)\n\n        return res.status(400).json({ error: "Recovery code required" });\n\n      const normalized = normalizeRecoveryCode(recoveryCode);\n\n      const codes = db.getUnusedRecoveryCodes(user.id);\n\n      let matched = null;\n\n      for (const c of codes) {\n\n        if (await argon2.verify(c.code_hash, normalized)) {\n\n          matched = c;\n\n          break;\n\n        }\n\n      }\n\n      if (!matched)\n\n        return res.status(401).json({ error: "Invalid recovery code" });\n\n      db.markRecoveryCodeUsed(matched.id);\n\n    }\n\n\n\n    const hash = await argon2.hash(newPassword);\n\n    db.updateUserPassword(user.id, hash);\n\n    db.setMustChangePassword(user.id, false);\n\n    res.status(200).json({ success: true });\n\n  } catch (e) {\n\n    res.status(500).json({ error: "Internal server error" });\n\n  }\n\n});\n\n\n\n// --- ADMIN PROFILE ---\n\napp.get("/api/admin/profile", requireAuth, (req, res) => {\n\n  const user = db.findUserById(req.session.userId);\n\n  const codes = db.getUnusedRecoveryCodes(user.id);\n\n  res.json({\n\n    id: user.id,\n\n    username: user.username,\n\n    email: user.email,\n\n    full_name: user.full_name,\n\n    role: user.role,\n\n    has2FA: !!user.totp_secret,\n\n    recoveryCodesRemaining: codes.length,\n\n    mustChangePassword: !!user.must_change_password,\n\n  });\n\n});\n\n\n\napp.put("/api/admin/profile", requireAuth, (req, res) => {\n\n  db.updateUserProfile(req.session.userId, req.body.email, req.body.full_name);\n\n  res.json({ success: true });\n\n});\n\n\n\napp.post("/api/admin/change-password", requireAuth, async (req, res) => {\n\n  try {\n\n    const { currentPassword, newPassword, totpToken } = req.body;\n\n    if (!currentPassword || !newPassword)\n\n      return res\n\n        .status(400)\n\n        .json({ error: "Current and new password required" });\n\n    if (newPassword.length < 8)\n\n      return res\n\n        .status(400)\n\n        .json({ error: "Password must be at least 8 characters" });\n\n    const user = db.findUserById(req.session.userId);\n\n    const ok = await argon2.verify(user.password_hash, currentPassword);\n\n    if (!ok)\n\n      return res.status(401).json({ error: "Current password is incorrect" });\n\n    if (user.totp_secret) {\n\n      if (!totpToken)\n\n        return res.status(400).json({ error: "2FA code required" });\n\n      const v = speakeasy.totp.verify({\n\n        secret: user.totp_secret,\n\n        encoding: "base32",\n\n        token: totpToken,\n\n        window: 1,\n\n      });\n\n      if (!v) return res.status(401).json({ error: "Invalid 2FA code" });\n\n    }\n\n    db.updateUserPassword(user.id, await argon2.hash(newPassword));\n\n    db.setMustChangePassword(user.id, false);\n\n    res.status(200).json({ success: true });\n\n  } catch (e) {\n\n    res.status(500).json({ error: "Internal server error" });\n\n  }\n\n});\n\n\n\napp.post(\n\n  "/api/admin/recovery-codes/regenerate",\n\n  requireAuth,\n\n  async (req, res) => {\n\n    try {\n\n      const { currentPassword } = req.body;\n\n      if (!currentPassword)\n\n        return res.status(400).json({ error: "Current password required" });\n\n      const user = db.findUserById(req.session.userId);\n\n      const ok = await argon2.verify(user.password_hash, currentPassword);\n\n      if (!ok)\n\n        return res.status(401).json({ error: "Current password is incorrect" });\n\n      const generated = await db.issueRecoveryCodes(user.id);\n\n      res.status(200).json({ success: true, codes: generated });\n\n    } catch (e) {\n\n      res.status(500).json({ error: "Internal server error" });\n\n    }\n\n  },\n\n);\n\n\n\napp.post("/api/admin/2fa/setup", requireAuth, (req, res) => {\n\n  const user = db.findUserById(req.session.userId);\n\n  const secret = speakeasy.generateSecret({\n\n    name: `DroneAdmin (${user.username})`,\n\n  });\n\n  qrcode.toDataURL(secret.otpauth_url, (err, url) => {\n\n    if (err) return res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to generate QR" });\n\n    res.json({ secret: secret.base32, qrCode: url });\n\n  });\n\n});\n\n\n\napp.post("/api/admin/2fa/enable", requireAuth, (req, res) => {\n\n  const ok = speakeasy.totp.verify({\n\n    secret: req.body.secret,\n\n    encoding: "base32",\n\n    token: req.body.token,\n\n  });\n\n  if (ok) {\n\n    db.updateUserTotp(req.session.userId, req.body.secret);\n\n    res.json({ success: true });\n\n  } else {\n\n    res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid code." });\n\n  }\n\n});\n\n\n\n// --- ADMIN USER MANAGEMENT (owner only) ---\n\n// Lets the owner create additional admin accounts (e.g. for alpha testers) without\n\n// shell access to run create-admin.js. New accounts always get the "admin" role,\n\n// a random one-time temporary password, and must_change_password = true, so the\n\n// owner never has to choose or know their long-term password.\n\nconst USERNAME_RE = /^[a-zA-Z0-9._-]{3,32}$/;\n\n\n\napp.get("/api/admin/users", requireAuth, requireOwner, (req, res) => {\n\n  res.json(db.getAllUsers());\n\n});\n\n\n\napp.post("/api/admin/users", requireAuth, requireOwner, async (req, res) => {\n\n  try {\n\n    const username = (req.body.username || "").trim();\n\n    const email = (req.body.email || "").trim();\n\n    const fullName = (req.body.full_name || "").trim() || null;\n\n\n\n    if (!username || !email)\n\n      return res.status(400).json({ error: "Username and email are required" });\n\n    if (!USERNAME_RE.test(username))\n\n      return res.status(400).json({\n\n        error:\n\n          "Username must be 3-32 characters: letters, numbers, dot, underscore, or hyphen only",\n\n      });\n\n    if (!email.includes("@"))\n\n      return res.status(400).json({ error: "A valid email is required" });\n\n    if (db.findUserByUsername(username))\n\n      return res.status(400).json({ error: "Username already taken" });\n\n\n\n    const tempPassword = db.generateTempPassword();\n\n    const passwordHash = await argon2.hash(tempPassword);\n\n    db.createUser(username, passwordHash, email, fullName, "admin", true);\n\n    const user = db.findUserByUsername(username);\n\n    const recoveryCodes = await db.issueRecoveryCodes(user.id);\n\n\n\n    res.status(201).json({\n\n      success: true,\n\n      user: {\n\n        id: user.id,\n\n        username: user.username,\n\n        email: user.email,\n\n        full_name: user.full_name,\n\n        role: user.role,\n\n      },\n\n      tempPassword,\n\n      recoveryCodes,\n\n    });\n\n  } catch (e) {\n\n    if (e.code === "SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE")\n\n      return res.status(400).json({ error: "Username already taken" });\n\n    res.status(500).json({ error: "Internal server error" });\n\n  }\n\n});\n\n\n\n// Re-issues a one-time temporary password and recovery codes for an account that\'s\n\n// locked out, and clears any 2FA so the owner isn\'t locked out of locking them out.\n\n// Owner accounts aren\'t resettable here - they go through the CLI script or the\n\n// normal self-service "Forgot password" flow instead.\n\napp.post(\n\n  "/api/admin/users/:id/reset-password",\n\n  requireAuth,\n\n  requireOwner,\n\n  async (req, res) => {\n\n    try {\n\n      const target = db.findUserById(req.params.id);\n\n      if (!target) return res.status(404).json({ error: "User not found" });\n\n      if (target.role === "owner")\n\n        return res.status(400).json({\n\n          error: "Owner accounts can\'t be reset here - use Settings or the CLI script.",\n\n        });\n\n\n\n      const tempPassword = db.generateTempPassword();\n\n      db.updateUserPassword(target.id, await argon2.hash(tempPassword));\n\n      db.updateUserTotp(target.id, null);\n\n      db.setMustChangePassword(target.id, true);\n\n      const recoveryCodes = await db.issueRecoveryCodes(target.id);\n\n\n\n      res.json({ success: true, tempPassword, recoveryCodes });\n\n    } catch (e) {\n\n      res.status(500).json({ error: "Internal server error" });\n\n    }\n\n  },\n\n);\n\n\n\napp.delete("/api/admin/users/:id", requireAuth, requireOwner, (req, res) => {\n\n  try {\n\n    const targetId = Number(req.params.id);\n\n    if (targetId === req.session.userId)\n\n      return res.status(400).json({ error: "You cannot delete your own account" });\n\n    const target = db.findUserById(targetId);\n\n    if (!target) return res.status(404).json({ error: "User not found" });\n\n    if (target.role === "owner")\n\n      return res.status(400).json({ error: "Owner accounts can\'t be deleted here" });\n\n    db.deleteUser(targetId);\n\n    res.json({ success: true });\n\n  } catch (e) {\n\n    res.status(500).json({ error: "Internal server error" });\n\n  }\n\n});\n\n\n\n// --- CRM ---\n\napp.get("/api/admin/inquiries", requireAuth, (req, res) =>\n\n  res.json(db.getAllInquiries()),\n\n);\n\napp.put("/api/admin/inquiries/:id", requireAuth, (req, res) => {\n\n  const { status, notes } = req.body;\n\n  if (!["New", "Contacted", "Quoted", "Closed"].includes(status))\n\n    return res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid status" });\n\n  db.updateInquiry(req.params.id, status, notes || "");\n\n  res.json({ success: true });\n\n});\n\napp.delete("/api/admin/inquiries/:id", requireAuth, (req, res) => {\n\n  try {\n\n    db.deleteInquiry(req.params.id);\n\n    res.json({ success: true });\n\n  } catch (e) {\n\n    res.status(500).json({ error: "Internal server error" });\n\n  }\n\n});\n\napp.get("/api/admin/customers", requireAuth, (req, res) =>\n\n  res.json(db.getAllCustomers()),\n\n);\n\napp.get("/api/admin/customers/:id", requireAuth, (req, res) => {\n\n  const data = db.getCustomerFullProfile(req.params.id);\n\n  if (!data) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Not found" });\n\n  res.json(data);\n\n});\n\napp.put("/api/admin/customers/:id", requireAuth, (req, res) => {\n\n  const { name, email, company, phone, address, notes } = req.body;\n\n  try {\n\n    db.updateCustomer(\n\n      req.params.id,\n\n      name,\n\n      email,\n\n      company,\n\n      phone,\n\n      address,\n\n      notes,\n\n    );\n\n    res.json({ success: true });\n\n  } catch (e) {\n\n    if (e.code === "SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE")\n\n      return res\n\n        .status(400)\n\n        .json({ error: "Email already assigned to another customer." });\n\n    res.status(500).json({ error: "Internal server error" });\n\n  }\n\n});\n\napp.delete("/api/admin/customers/:id", requireAuth, (req, res) => {\n\n  try {\n\n    db.deleteCustomer(req.params.id);\n\n    res.json({ success: true });\n\n  } catch (e) {\n\n    res.status(500).json({ error: "Internal server error" });\n\n  }\n\n});\n\napp.post("/api/admin/customers/:id/jobs", requireAuth, (req, res) => {\n\n  const { title, status, date, price, notes } = req.body;\n\n  if (!title) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Job title required" });\n\n  db.addJob(req.params.id, title, status || "Scheduled", date, price, notes);\n\n  res.json({ success: true });\n\n});\n\napp.delete("/api/admin/jobs/:id", requireAuth, (req, res) => {\n\n  try {\n\n    db.deleteJob(req.params.id);\n\n    res.json({ success: true });\n\n  } catch (e) {\n\n    res.status(500).json({ error: "Internal server error" });\n\n  }\n\n});\n\n\n\n// --- PUBLIC CONTACT ---\n\napp.post("/api/contact", contactLimiter, (req, res) => {\n\n  const { name, email, subject, message, website, referral_source } = req.body;\n\n  if (website) return res.json({ success: true });\n\n  if (!name || !email || !subject || !message || !email.includes("@"))\n\n    return res.status(400).json({ error: "Valid data required" });\n\n  try {\n\n    const customer = db.getOrCreateCustomer(name, email);\n\n    db.insertInquiry(\n\n      customer.id,\n\n      name,\n\n      email,\n\n      subject,\n\n      message,\n\n      req.ip,\n\n      referral_source,\n\n    );\n\n    res.json({ success: true });\n\n  } catch (e) {\n\n    res.status(500).json({ error: "Internal server error" });\n\n  }\n\n});\n\n\n\n// --- STANDARD EXPORTS (date range) ---\n\napp.get("/api/admin/export/inquiries", requireAuth, (req, res) => {\n\n  try {\n\n    const { startDate, endDate } = req.query;\n\n    const inquiries = db.getFilteredInquiries(startDate, endDate);\n\n    const csv = jsonToCsv(inquiries, [\n\n      { key: "id", label: "ID" },\n\n      { key: "created_at", label: "Date" },\n\n      { key: "name", label: "Name" },\n\n      { key: "email", label: "Email" },\n\n      { key: "subject", label: "Subject" },\n\n      { key: "status", label: "Status" },\n\n      { key: "referral_source", label: "Source" },\n\n      { key: "ip_address", label: "IP Address" },\n\n    ]);\n\n    res.setHeader("Content-Type", "text/csv");\n\n    res.setHeader(\n\n      "Content-Disposition",\n\n      `attachment; filename="inquiries_${startDate || "all"}_${endDate || "all"}.csv"`,\n\n    );\n\n    res.send(csv);\n\n  } catch (e) {\n\n    res.status(500).json({ error: "Export failed" });\n\n  }\n\n});\n\n\n\napp.get("/api/admin/export/jobs", requireAuth, (req, res) => {\n\n  try {\n\n    const { startDate, endDate } = req.query;\n\n    const jobs = db.getFilteredJobs(startDate, endDate);\n\n    const csv = jsonToCsv(jobs, [\n\n      { key: "id", label: "Job ID" },\n\n      { key: "customer_name", label: "Customer" },\n\n      { key: "customer_email", label: "Email" },\n\n      { key: "title", label: "Title" },\n\n      { key: "date", label: "Date" },\n\n      { key: "status", label: "Status" },\n\n      { key: "price", label: "Price (USD)" },\n\n    ]);\n\n    res.setHeader("Content-Type", "text/csv");\n\n    res.setHeader(\n\n      "Content-Disposition",\n\n      `attachment; filename="jobs_${startDate || "all"}_${endDate || "all"}.csv"`,\n\n    );\n\n    res.send(csv);\n\n  } catch (e) {\n\n    res.status(500).json({ error: "Export failed" });\n\n  }\n\n});\n\n\n\napp.get("/api/admin/export/stats", requireAuth, (req, res) => {\n\n  try {\n\n    const { startDate, endDate } = req.query;\n\n    const stats = db.getFilteredCustomerStats(startDate, endDate);\n\n    const csv = jsonToCsv(stats, [\n\n      { key: "customer_id", label: "Customer ID" },\n\n      { key: "name", label: "Name" },\n\n      { key: "email", label: "Email" },\n\n      { key: "total_jobs", label: "Total Jobs" },\n\n      { key: "total_revenue", label: "Revenue (USD)" },\n\n    ]);\n\n    res.setHeader("Content-Type", "text/csv");\n\n    res.setHeader(\n\n      "Content-Disposition",\n\n      `attachment; filename="stats_${startDate || "all"}_${endDate || "all"}.csv"`,\n\n    );\n\n    res.send(csv);\n\n  } catch (e) {\n\n    res.status(500).json({ error: "Export failed" });\n\n  }\n\n});\n\n\n\n// --- SCHEMA & CUSTOM QUERY BUILDER ---\n\napp.get("/api/admin/schema", requireAuth, (req, res) => {\n\n  res.json(SCHEMA);\n\n});\n\n\n\napp.post("/api/admin/query/preview", requireAuth, (req, res) => {\n\n  try {\n\n    const { spec } = req.body;\n\n    if (!spec) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Missing query spec" });\n\n    const { sql, params, headers } = buildQuery(spec);\n\n    const rows = db.runStructuredQuery(sql, params);\n\n    res.json({ rows, headers, sql, params }); // returning sql/params is useful for debugging\n\n  } catch (e) {\n\n    res.status(400).json({ error: e.message });\n\n  }\n\n});\n\n\n\napp.post("/api/admin/query/export", requireAuth, (req, res) => {\n\n  try {\n\n    const { spec } = req.body;\n\n    if (!spec) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Missing query spec" });\n\n    const { sql, params, headers } = buildQuery(spec);\n\n    const rows = db.runStructuredQuery(sql, params);\n\n    const csv = jsonToCsv(rows, headers);\n\n    res.setHeader("Content-Type", "text/csv");\n\n    res.setHeader(\n\n      "Content-Disposition",\n\n      `attachment; filename="custom_report_${Date.now()}.csv"`,\n\n    );\n\n    res.send(csv);\n\n  } catch (e) {\n\n    res.status(400).json({ error: e.message });\n\n  }\n\n});\n\n\n\napp.post("/api/admin/query/raw", requireAuth, (req, res) => {\n\n  try {\n\n    const { sql, format = "json" } = req.body;\n\n    if (!sql) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Missing SQL" });\n\n    const { rows, headers } = runRawQuery(sql);\n\n    if (format === "csv") {\n\n      const csv = jsonToCsv(rows, headers);\n\n      res.setHeader("Content-Type", "text/csv");\n\n      res.setHeader(\n\n        "Content-Disposition",\n\n        `attachment; filename="raw_query_${Date.now()}.csv"`,\n\n      );\n\n      return res.send(csv);\n\n    }\n\n    res.json({ rows, headers });\n\n  } catch (e) {\n\n    res.status(400).json({ error: e.message });\n\n  }\n\n});\n\n\n\n// --- UTILITY ---\n\napp.get("/health", (req, res) => res.json({ status: "healthy" }));\n\napp.get("/assets/optimized/:filename", (req, res) => {\n\n  const filename = req.params.filename;\n\n  const opt = path.join(__dirname, "assets", "optimized", filename);\n\n  fs.access(opt, fs.constants.F_OK, (err) => {\n\n    if (!err)\n\n      return res.sendFile(opt, {\n\n        headers: { "Cache-Control": "public, max-age=31536000" },\n\n      });\n\n    const orig = path.join(__dirname, "assets", "images", filename);\n\n    fs.access(orig, fs.constants.F_OK, (oErr) => {\n\n      if (!oErr)\n\n        return res.sendFile(orig, {\n\n          headers: { "Cache-Control": "public, max-age=31536000" },\n\n        });\n\n      return res.status(404).json({ error: "File not found" });\n\n    });\n\n  });\n\n});\n\n\n\napp.use((req, res) =>\n\n  res.status(404).sendFile(path.join(__dirname, "index.html")),\n\n);\n\napp.use((err, req, res, next) => {\n\n  console.error("Unhandled error:", err);\n\n  res.status(500).json({ error: "Internal server error" });\n\n});\n\n\n\napp.listen(PORT, () => {\n\n  console.log(`🚀 Server running on http://localhost:${PORT}`);\n\n});\n\n\n=== END server.js WHERE id = ?");\n\n  stmt.run(status, notes, id);\n\n}\n\n\n\nfunction deleteInquiry(id) {\n\n  const db = getDb();\n\n  const stmt = db.prepare("DELETE FROM inquiries WHERE id = ?");\n\n  stmt.run(id);\n\n}\n\n\n\nfunction getAllCustomers() {\n\n  const db = getDb();\n\n  return db.prepare("SELECT * FROM customers ORDER BY created_at DESC").all();\n\n}\n\n\n\nfunction getOrCreateCustomer(name, email) {\n\n  const db = getDb();\n\n  \n\n  // Try to find existing customer\n\n  let customer = db.prepare("SELECT id FROM customers WHERE email = ?").get(email);\n\n  \n\n  if (!customer) {\n\n    // Create new customer\n\n    const stmt = db.prepare("INSERT INTO customers (name, email) VALUES (?, ?)");\n\n    const result = stmt.run(name, email);\n\n    customer = { id: result.lastInsertRowid };\n\n  }\n\n  \n\n  return customer;\n\n}\n\n\n\nfunction getCustomerFullProfile(customerId) {\n\n  const db = getDb();\n\n  const profile = db.prepare(\n\n    `\n\n      SELECT c.*, \n\n             COUNT(j.id) as total_jobs,\n\n             SUM(j.price) as total_revenue\n\n      FROM customers c\n\n      LEFT JOIN jobs j ON c.id = j.customer_id\n\n      WHERE c.id = ?\n\n      GROUP BY c.id\n\n    `,\n\n  ).get(customerId);\n\n  \n\n  if (!profile) return null;\n\n  \n\n  // Add job details to profile\n\n  const jobs = db.prepare(\n\n    `\n\n      SELECT * FROM jobs \n\n      WHERE customer_id = ? \n\n      ORDER BY created_at DESC\n\n    `,\n\n  ).all(customerId);\n\n  \n\n  profile.jobs = jobs;\n\n  return profile;\n\n}\n\n\n\nfunction updateCustomer(id, name, email, company, phone, address, notes) {\n\n  const db = getDb();\n\n  const stmt = db.prepare(\n\n    "UPDATE customers SET name = ?, email = ?, company = ?, phone = ?, address = ?, notes = ? AND ?\n\n        GROUP BY c.id\n\n        ORDER BY total_revenue DESC\n\n      `,\n\n    ).all(startDate, endDate);\n\n  } else if (startDate) {\n\n    return db.prepare(\n\n      `\n\n        SELECT \n\n          c.id as customer_id,\n\n          c.name,\n\n          c.email,\n\n          COUNT(j.id) as total_jobs,\n\n          SUM(j.price) as total_revenue\n\n        FROM customers c\n\n        LEFT JOIN jobs j ON c.id = j.customer_id\n\n        WHERE j.created_at >= ?\n\n        GROUP BY c.id\n\n        ORDER BY total_revenue DESC\n\n      `,\n\n    ).all(startDate);\n\n  } else if (endDate) {\n\n    return db.prepare(\n\n      `\n\n        SELECT \n\n          c.id as customer_id,\n\n          c.name,\n\n          c.email,\n\n          COUNT(j.id) as total_jobs,\n\n          SUM(j.price) as total_revenue\n\n        FROM customers c\n\n        LEFT JOIN jobs j ON c.id = j.customer_id\n\n        WHERE j.created_at <= ?\n\n        GROUP BY c.id\n\n        ORDER BY total_revenue DESC\n\n      `,\n\n    ).all(endDate);\n\n  }\n\n  \n\n  return db.prepare(\n\n    `\n\n      SELECT \n\n        c.id as customer_id,\n\n        c.name,\n\n        c.email,\n\n        COUNT(j.id) as total_jobs,\n\n        SUM(j.price) as total_revenue\n\n      FROM customers c\n\n      LEFT JOIN jobs j ON c.id = j.customer_id\n\n      GROUP BY c.id\n\n      ORDER BY total_revenue DESC\n\n    `,\n\n  ).all();\n\n}\n\n\n\nfunction getCustomerStats(customerId) {\n\n  const db = getDb();\n\n  \n\n  return db.prepare(\n\n    `\n\n      SELECT \n\n        c.id as customer_id,\n\n        c.name,\n\n        c.email,\n\n        COUNT(j.id) as total_jobs,\n\n        SUM(j.price) as total_revenue\n\n      FROM customers c\n\n      LEFT JOIN jobs j ON c.id = j.customer_id\n\n      WHERE c.id = ?\n\n      GROUP BY c.id\n\n    `,\n\n  ).get(customerId);\n\n}\n\n\n\n// Export all functions\n\nmodule.exports = {\n\n  initInquiriesDB,\n\n  getAllInquiries,\n\n  insertInquiry,\n\n  updateInquiry,\n\n  deleteInquiry,\n\n  getAllCustomers,\n\n  getOrCreateCustomer,\n\n  getCustomerFullProfile,\n\n  updateCustomer,\n\n  deleteCustomer,\n\n  addJob,\n\n  deleteJob,\n\n  findUserById,\n\n  findUserByUsername,\n\n  createUser,\n\n  updateUserPassword,\n\n  updateUserTotp,\n\n  setMustChangePassword,\n\n  generateTempPassword,\n\n  issueRecoveryCodes,\n\n  getUnusedRecoveryCodes,\n\n  markRecoveryCodeUsed,\n\n  updateLastLogin,\n\n  getFilteredInquiries,\n\n  getFilteredJobs,\n\n  getFilteredCustomerStats,\n\n  getCustomerStats,\n\n  runRawQuery,\n\n};\n\n\n\n// Export SCHEMA for schema inspection\n\nmodule.exports.SCHEMA = SCHEMA;\n\n=== END db.js ===\n\n### USER\nThe runtime fetched the files you requested. Continue your analysis.\n\n\n=== db.js ===\nconst Database = require("better-sqlite3");\n\nconst path = require("path");\n\nconst crypto = require("crypto");\n\nconst argon2 = require("argon2");\n\n\n\nconst dbPath = path.join(__dirname, "data", "inquiries.db");\n\nconst db = new Database(dbPath, { verbose: console.log });\n\n\n\ndb.pragma("foreign_keys = ON");\n\n\n\n// ==========================================\n\n// MIGRATIONS & SCHEMA\n\n// ==========================================\n\ndb.exec(`\n\n  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS Customers (\n\n    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n\n    name TEXT NOT NULL,\n\n    email TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,\n\n    company TEXT,\n\n    phone TEXT,\n\n    address TEXT,\n\n    notes TEXT DEFAULT \'\',\n\n    last_contacted_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n\n    created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP\n\n  )\n\n`);\n\n\n\ndb.exec(`\n\n  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS Inquiries (\n\n    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n\n    customer_id INTEGER,\n\n    name TEXT NOT NULL,\n\n    email TEXT NOT NULL,\n\n    subject TEXT NOT NULL,\n\n    message TEXT NOT NULL,\n\n    status TEXT DEFAULT \'New\',\n\n    notes TEXT DEFAULT \'\',\n\n    ip_address TEXT,\n\n    referral_source TEXT,\n\n    created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n\n    FOREIGN KEY(customer_id) REFERENCES Customers(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n\n  )\n\n`);\n\n\n\ndb.exec(`\n\n  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS Jobs (\n\n    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n\n    customer_id INTEGER,\n\n    title TEXT NOT NULL,\n\n    status TEXT DEFAULT \'Scheduled\',\n\n    date TEXT,\n\n    price REAL DEFAULT 0,\n\n    notes TEXT DEFAULT \'\',\n\n    created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n\n    FOREIGN KEY(customer_id) REFERENCES Customers(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n\n  )\n\n`);\n\n\n\ndb.exec(`\n\n  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS Users (\n\n    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n\n    username TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,\n\n    password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,\n\n    email TEXT,\n\n    full_name TEXT,\n\n    role TEXT DEFAULT \'admin\',\n\n    totp_secret TEXT,\n\n    last_login TEXT,\n\n    created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP\n\n  )\n\n`);\n\n\n\ndb.exec(`\n\n  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS RecoveryCodes (\n\n    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n\n    user_id INTEGER NOT NULL,\n\n    code_hash TEXT NOT NULL,\n\n    used_at TEXT,\n\n    created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n\n    FOREIGN KEY(user_id) REFERENCES Users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n\n  )\n\n`);\n\n\n\nconst addColumnIfMissing = (table, column, type) => {\n\n  const info = db.pragma(`table_info(${table})`);\n\n  if (!info.find((c) => c.name === column))\n\n    db.exec(`ALTER TABLE ${table} ADD COLUMN ${column} ${type}`);\n\n};\n\n\n\naddColumnIfMissing("Customers", "address", "TEXT");\n\naddColumnIfMissing("Customers", "last_contacted_at", "TEXT");\n\naddColumnIfMissing("Inquiries", "ip_address", "TEXT");\n\naddColumnIfMissing("Inquiries", "referral_source", "TEXT");\n\naddColumnIfMissing("Jobs", "price", "REAL DEFAULT 0");\naddColumnIfMissing("Users", "must_change_password", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0");\n\n\n\ndb.exec(\n\n  `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_inquiries_customer ON Inquiries(customer_id);`,\n\n);\n\ndb.exec(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_jobs_customer ON Jobs(customer_id);`);\n\ndb.exec(\n\n  `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_inquiries_status ON Inquiries(status);`,\n\n);\n\n\n\ndb.exec(`\n\n  CREATE VIEW IF NOT EXISTS CustomerStats AS\n\n  SELECT c.id as customer_id, c.name, c.email,\n\n         COUNT(j.id) as total_jobs, COALESCE(SUM(j.price), 0) as total_revenue\n\n  FROM Customers c LEFT JOIN Jobs j ON c.id = j.customer_id\n\n  GROUP BY c.id\n\n`);\n\n\n\ndb.exec(`\n\n  CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS update_customer_contact_inquiry\n\n  AFTER INSERT ON Inquiries BEGIN\n\n    UPDATE Customers SET last_contacted_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE id = NEW.customer_id;\n\n  END\n\n`);\n\n\n\ndb.exec(`\n\n  CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS update_customer_contact_job\n\n  AFTER INSERT ON Jobs BEGIN\n\n    UPDATE Customers SET last_contacted_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE id = NEW.customer_id;\n\n  END\n\n`);\n\n\n\n// ==========================================\n\n// QUERIES\n\n// ==========================================\n\nfunction getOrCreateCustomer(name, email, company = null, phone = null) {\n\n  const normalizedEmail = email.toLowerCase().trim();\n\n  let customer = db\n\n    .prepare("SELECT * FROM Customers WHERE email = ?")\n\n    .get(normalizedEmail);\n\n  if (!customer) {\n\n    const info = db\n\n      .prepare(\n\n        "INSERT INTO Customers (name, email, company, phone) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",\n\n      )\n\n      .run(name, normalizedEmail, company, phone);\n\n    customer = db\n\n      .prepare("SELECT * FROM Customers WHERE id = ?")\n\n      .get(info.lastInsertRowid);\n\n  }\n\n  return customer;\n\n}\n\nfunction getAllCustomers() {\n\n  return db\n\n    .prepare("SELECT * FROM Customers ORDER BY last_contacted_at DESC")\n\n    .all();\n\n}\n\nfunction getCustomerFullProfile(id) {\n\n  const customer = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM Customers WHERE id = ?").get(id);\n\n  if (!customer) return null;\n\n  customer.inquiries = db\n\n    .prepare(\n\n      "SELECT * FROM Inquiries WHERE customer_id = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC",\n\n    )\n\n    .all(id);\n\n  customer.jobs = db\n\n    .prepare(\n\n      "SELECT * FROM Jobs WHERE customer_id = ? ORDER BY date DESC, created_at DESC",\n\n    )\n\n    .all(id);\n\n  return customer;\n\n}\n\nfunction updateCustomer(id, name, email, company, phone, address, notes) {\n\n  return db\n\n    .prepare(\n\n      "UPDATE Customers SET name = ?, email = ?, company = ?, phone = ?, address = ?, notes = ? WHERE id = ?",\n\n    )\n\n    .run(name, email.toLowerCase().trim(), company, phone, address, notes, id);\n\n}\n\nfunction deleteCustomer(id) {\n\n  return db.prepare("DELETE FROM Customers WHERE id = ?").run(id);\n\n}\n\nfunction addJob(customerId, title, status, date, price, notes) {\n\n  return db\n\n    .prepare(\n\n      "INSERT INTO Jobs (customer_id, title, status, date, price, notes) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",\n\n    )\n\n    .run(customerId, title, status, date, price || 0, notes);\n\n}\n\nfunction deleteJob(id) {\n\n  return db.prepare("DELETE FROM Jobs WHERE id = ?").run(id);\n\n}\n\nfunction insertInquiry(\n\n  customerId,\n\n  name,\n\n  email,\n\n  subject,\n\n  message,\n\n  ipAddress,\n\n  referralSource,\n\n) {\n\n  return db\n\n    .prepare(\n\n      "INSERT INTO Inquiries (customer_id, name, email, subject, message, ip_address, referral_source, status) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, \'New\')",\n\n    )\n\n    .run(customerId, name, email, subject, message, ipAddress, referralSource);\n\n}\n\nfunction getAllInquiries() {\n\n  return db\n\n    .prepare(\n\n      "SELECT i.*, c.company FROM Inquiries i LEFT JOIN Customers c ON i.customer_id = c.id ORDER BY i.created_at DESC",\n\n    )\n\n    .all();\n\n}\n\nfunction updateInquiry(id, status, notes) {\n\n  return db\n\n    .prepare("UPDATE Inquiries SET status = ?, notes = ? WHERE id = ?")\n\n    .run(status, notes, id);\n\n}\n\nfunction deleteInquiry(id) {\n\n  return db.prepare("DELETE FROM Inquiries WHERE id = ?").run(id);\n\n}\n\n\n\nfunction findUserByUsername(username) {\n\n  return db.prepare("SELECT * FROM Users WHERE username = ?").get(username);\n\n}\n\nfunction findUserById(id) {\n\n  return db.prepare("SELECT * FROM Users WHERE id = ?").get(id);\n\n}\n\nfunction updateUserProfile(id, email, fullName) {\n\n  return db\n\n    .prepare("UPDATE Users SET email = ?, full_name = ? WHERE id = ?")\n\n    .run(email, fullName, id);\n\n}\n\nfunction updateUserTotp(id, secret) {\n\n  return db\n\n    .prepare("UPDATE Users SET totp_secret = ? WHERE id = ?")\n\n    .run(secret, id);\n\n}\n\nfunction updateUserPassword(id, passwordHash) {\n\n  return db\n\n    .prepare("UPDATE Users SET password_hash = ? WHERE id = ?")\n\n    .run(passwordHash, id);\n\n}\n\nfunction updateLastLogin(id) {\n\n  return db\n\n    .prepare("UPDATE Users SET last_login = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE id = ?")\n\n    .run(id);\n\n}\n\nfunction createUser(\n\n  username,\n\n  passwordHash,\n\n  email = null,\n\n  fullName = null,\n\n  role = "admin",\n\n  mustChangePassword = false,\n\n) {\n\n  return db\n\n    .prepare(\n\n      "INSERT INTO Users (username, password_hash, email, full_name, role, must_change_password) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",\n\n    )\n\n    .run(\n\n      username,\n\n      passwordHash,\n\n      email,\n\n      fullName,\n\n      role,\n\n      mustChangePassword ? 1 : 0,\n\n    );\n\n}\n\nfunction getAllUsers() {\n\n  // Deliberately excludes password_hash and totp_secret - never send secrets to the client.\n\n  return db\n\n    .prepare(\n\n      "SELECT id, username, email, full_name, role, must_change_password, last_login, created_at FROM Users ORDER BY created_at ASC",\n\n    )\n\n    .all();\n\n}\n\nfunction deleteUser(id) {\n\n  return db.prepare("DELETE FROM Users WHERE id = ?").run(id);\n\n}\n\nfunction setUserRole(id, role) {\n\n  return db.prepare("UPDATE Users SET role = ? WHERE id = ?").run(role, id);\n\n}\n\nfunction setMustChangePassword(id, value) {\n\n  return db\n\n    .prepare("UPDATE Users SET must_change_password = ? WHERE id = ?")\n\n    .run(value ? 1 : 0, id);\n\n}\n\n\n\nfunction createRecoveryCode(userId, codeHash) {\n\n  return db\n\n    .prepare("INSERT INTO RecoveryCodes (user_id, code_hash) VALUES (?, ?)")\n\n    .run(userId, codeHash);\n\n}\n\nfunction getUnusedRecoveryCodes(userId) {\n\n  return db\n\n    .prepare(\n\n      "SELECT * FROM RecoveryCodes WHERE user_id = ? AND used_at IS NULL ORDER BY id",\n\n    )\n\n    .all(userId);\n\n}\n\nfunction markRecoveryCodeUsed(id) {\n\n  return db\n\n    .prepare(\n\n      "UPDATE RecoveryCodes SET used_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE id = ?",\n\n    )\n\n    .run(id);\n\n}\n\nfunction deleteAllRecoveryCodes(userId) {\n\n  return db.prepare("DELETE FROM RecoveryCodes WHERE user_id = ?").run(userId);\n\n}\n\n\n\n// Alphabet excludes I, O, 0, 1 to avoid transcription errors when codes are read aloud or copied.\n\nconst RECOVERY_CODE_ALPHABET = "ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789";\n\nfunction generateRecoveryCode() {\n\n  let raw = "";\n\n  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {\n\n    raw += RECOVERY_CODE_ALPHABET[crypto.randomInt(0, RECOVERY_CODE_ALPHABET.length)];\n\n  }\n\n  return { display: `${raw.slice(0, 4)}-${raw.slice(4)}`, normalized: raw };\n\n}\n\n// Wipes any existing codes and issues a fresh set of 8, returning the display strings\n\n// ONCE - only the argon2 hashes are persisted, so callers must hand these to the user now.\n\nasync function issueRecoveryCodes(userId) {\n\n  deleteAllRecoveryCodes(userId);\n\n  const codes = [];\n\n  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {\n\n    const code = generateRecoveryCode();\n\n    const hash = await argon2.hash(code.normalized);\n\n    createRecoveryCode(userId, hash);\n\n    codes.push(code.display);\n\n  }\n\n  return codes;\n\n}\n\n// High-entropy one-time temporary password for newly created or reset accounts.\n\n// Recipients are always required to change it (must_change_password) before doing anything else.\n\nfunction generateTempPassword() {\n\n  return crypto.randomBytes(18).toString("base64url"); // 24 URL-safe chars, ~144 bits\n\n}\n\n\n\n// Reporting\n\nfunction getFilteredInquiries(startDate, endDate) {\n\n  let q =\n\n    "SELECT i.*, c.company FROM Inquiries i LEFT JOIN Customers c ON i.customer_id = c.id WHERE 1=1";\n\n  const p = [];\n\n  if (startDate) {\n\n    q += " AND i.created_at >= ?";\n\n    p.push(`${startDate} 00:00:00`);\n\n  }\n\n  if (endDate) {\n\n    q += " AND i.created_at <= ?";\n\n    p.push(`${endDate} 23:59:59`);\n\n  }\n\n  return db.prepare(q + " ORDER BY i.created_at DESC").all(...p);\n\n}\n\nfunction getFilteredJobs(startDate, endDate) {\n\n  let q =\n\n    "SELECT j.*, c.name as customer_name, c.email as customer_email FROM Jobs j JOIN Customers c ON j.customer_id = c.id WHERE 1=1";\n\n  const p = [];\n\n  if (startDate) {\n\n    q += " AND j.date >= ?";\n\n    p.push(startDate);\n\n  }\n\n  if (endDate) {\n\n    q += " AND j.date <= ?";\n\n    p.push(endDate);\n\n  }\n\n  return db.prepare(q + " ORDER BY j.date DESC").all(...p);\n\n}\n\nfunction getFilteredCustomerStats(startDate, endDate) {\n\n  let q = `SELECT c.id as customer_id, c.name, c.email, COUNT(j.id) as total_jobs, COALESCE(SUM(j.price), 0) as total_revenue\n\n           FROM Customers c LEFT JOIN Jobs j ON c.id = j.customer_id`;\n\n  const p = [];\n\n  if (startDate || endDate) {\n\n    q += " AND j.date IS NOT NULL";\n\n    if (startDate) {\n\n      q += " AND j.date >= ?";\n\n      p.push(startDate);\n\n    }\n\n    if (endDate) {\n\n      q += " AND j.date <= ?";\n\n      p.push(endDate);\n\n    }\n\n  }\n\n  return db.prepare(q + " GROUP BY c.id ORDER BY total_revenue DESC").all(...p);\n\n}\n\n\n\n/**\n\n * Run a fully-formed, parameterized SQL string produced by the query builder.\n\n * The query builder has already validated every identifier.\n\n */\n\nfunction runStructuredQuery(sql, params = []) {\n\n  return db.prepare(sql).all(...params);\n\n}\n\n\n\nmodule.exports = {\n\n  getOrCreateCustomer,\n\n  getAllCustomers,\n\n  getCustomerFullProfile,\n\n  updateCustomer,\n\n  deleteCustomer,\n\n  addJob,\n\n  deleteJob,\n\n  insertInquiry,\n\n  getAllInquiries,\n\n  updateInquiry,\n\n  deleteInquiry,\n\n  findUserByUsername,\n\n  findUserById,\n\n  updateUserProfile,\n\n  updateUserTotp,\n\n  updateUserPassword,\n\n  updateLastLogin,\n\n  createUser,\n\n  getAllUsers,\n\n  deleteUser,\n\n  setUserRole,\n\n  setMustChangePassword,\n\n  createRecoveryCode,\n\n  getUnusedRecoveryCodes,\n\n  markRecoveryCodeUsed,\n\n  deleteAllRecoveryCodes,\n\n  issueRecoveryCodes,\n\n  generateTempPassword,\n\n  getFilteredInquiries,\n\n  getFilteredJobs,\n\n  getFilteredCustomerStats,\n\n  runStructuredQuery,\n\n};\n\n\n=== END db.js ==='}
